    55	#[cfg(not(feature = "noop"))]
    56	/// Create a custom Tokio runtime used by the NAPI-RS.
    57	/// You can control the tokio runtime configuration by yourself.
    58	/// ### Example
    59	/// ```no_run
    60	/// use tokio::runtime::Builder;
    61	/// use napi::create_custom_tokio_runtime;
    62	///
    63	/// #[napi_derive::module_init]
    64	/// fn init() {
    65	///    let rt = Builder::new_multi_thread().enable_all().thread_stack_size(32 * 1024 * 1024).build().unwrap();
    66	///    create_custom_tokio_runtime(rt);
    67	/// }
    68	pub fn create_custom_tokio_runtime(rt: Runtime) {
    69	  USER_DEFINED_RT.get_or_init(move || RwLock::new(Some(rt)));
    70	  IS_USER_DEFINED_RT.get_or_init(|| true);
    71	}
    72	
    73	#[cfg(feature = "noop")]
    74	pub fn create_custom_tokio_runtime(_: Runtime) {}
    75	
    76	#[cfg(not(feature = "noop"))]
    77	/// Start the async runtime (Currently is tokio).
    78	///
    79	/// In Node.js native targets the async runtime will be dropped when Node env exits.
    80	/// But in Electron renderer process, the Node env will exits and recreate when the window reloads.
    81	/// So we need to ensure that the async runtime is initialized when the Node env is created.
    82	///
    83	/// In wasm targets, the async runtime will not been shutdown automatically due to the limitation of the wasm runtime.
    84	/// So, you need to call `shutdown_async_runtime` function to manually shutdown the async runtime.
    85	/// In some scenarios, you may want to start the async runtime again like in tests.
    86	pub fn start_async_runtime() {
    87	  if let Ok(mut rt) = RT.write() {
    88	    if rt.is_none() {
    89	      *rt = Some(create_runtime());
    90	    }
    91	  }
    92	}
    93	
    94	#[cfg(not(feature = "noop"))]
    95	pub fn shutdown_async_runtime() {
    96	  if let Some(rt) = RT.write().ok().and_then(|mut rt| rt.take()) {
    97	    rt.shutdown_background();
    98	  }
    99	}

   101	#[cfg(not(feature = "noop"))]
   102	/// Spawns a future onto the Tokio runtime.
   103	///
   104	/// Depending on where you use it, you should await or abort the future in your drop function.
   105	/// To avoid undefined behavior and memory corruptions.
   106	pub fn spawn<F>(fut: F) -> tokio::task::JoinHandle<F::Output>
   107	where
   108	  F: 'static + Send + Future<Output = ()>,
   109	{
   110	  RT.read()
   111	    .ok()
   112	    .and_then(|rt| rt.as_ref().map(|rt| rt.spawn(fut)))
   113	    .expect("Access tokio runtime failed in spawn")
   114	}
   115	
   116	#[cfg(not(feature = "noop"))]
   117	/// Runs a future to completion
   118	/// This is blocking, meaning that it pauses other execution until the future is complete,
   119	/// only use it when it is absolutely necessary, in other places use async functions instead.
   120	pub fn block_on<F: Future>(fut: F) -> F::Output {
   121	  RT.read()
   122	    .ok()
   123	    .and_then(|rt| rt.as_ref().map(|rt| rt.block_on(fut)))
   124	    .expect("Access tokio runtime failed in block_on")
   125	}

   131	pub fn block_on<F: Future>(_: F) -> F::Output {
   132	  unreachable!("noop feature is enabled, block_on is not available")
   133	}
   134	
   135	#[cfg(not(feature = "noop"))]
   136	/// spawn_blocking on the current Tokio runtime.
   137	pub fn spawn_blocking<F, R>(func: F) -> tokio::task::JoinHandle<R>
   138	where
   139	  F: FnOnce() -> R + Send + 'static,
   140	  R: Send + 'static,
   141	{
   142	  RT.read()
   143	    .ok()
   144	    .and_then(|rt| rt.as_ref().map(|rt| rt.spawn_blocking(func)))
   145	    .expect("Access tokio runtime failed in spawn_blocking")
   146	}

   148	#[cfg(not(feature = "noop"))]
   149	// This function's signature must be kept in sync with the one in lib.rs, otherwise napi
   150	// will fail to compile with the `tokio_rt` feature.
   151	#[cfg(not(feature = "noop"))]
   152	/// If the feature `tokio_rt` has been enabled this will enter the runtime context and
   153	/// then call the provided closure. Otherwise it will just call the provided closure.
   154	pub fn within_runtime_if_available<F: FnOnce() -> T, T>(f: F) -> T {
   155	  RT.read()
   156	    .ok()
   157	    .and_then(|rt| {
   158	      rt.as_ref().map(|rt| {
   159	        let rt_guard = rt.enter();
   160	        let ret = f();
   161	        drop(rt_guard);
   162	        ret
   163	      })
   164	    })
   165	    .expect("Access tokio runtime failed in within_runtime_if_available")
   166	}

   168	#[cfg(feature = "noop")]
   169	pub fn within_runtime_if_available<F: FnOnce() -> T, T>(f: F) -> T {
   170	  f()
   171	}

   178	  Resolver: 'static + FnOnce(sys::napi_env, Data) -> Result<sys::napi_value>,
   179	>(
   180	  env: sys::napi_env,

   186	
   187	#[cfg(not(feature = "noop"))]
   188	#[allow(clippy::not_unsafe_ptr_arg_deref)]
   189	pub fn execute_tokio_future<
   190	  Data: 'static + Send,
   191	  Fut: 'static + Send + Future<Output = std::result::Result<Data, impl Into<Error>>>,
   192	  Resolver: 'static + FnOnce(sys::napi_env, Data) -> Result<sys::napi_value>,
   193	>(
   194	  env: sys::napi_env,
   195	  fut: Fut,
   196	  resolver: Resolver,
   197	) -> Result<sys::napi_value> {
   198	  let env = Env::from_raw(env);
   199	  let (deferred, promise) = JsDeferred::new(&env)?;
   200	  #[cfg(any(
   201	    all(target_family = "wasm", tokio_unstable),
   202	    not(target_family = "wasm")
   203	  ))]
   204	  let deferred_for_panic = deferred.clone();
   205	  let sendable_resolver = SendableResolver::new(resolver);
   206	
   207	  let inner = async move {
   208	    match fut.await {
   209	      Ok(v) => deferred.resolve(move |env| {
   210	        sendable_resolver
   211	          .resolve(env.raw(), v)
   212	          .map(|v| unsafe { Unknown::from_raw_unchecked(env.raw(), v) })
   213	      }),
   214	      Err(e) => deferred.reject(e.into()),
   215	    }
   216	  };
   217	
   218	  #[cfg(any(
   219	    all(target_family = "wasm", tokio_unstable),
   220	    not(target_family = "wasm")
   221	  ))]
   222	  let jh = spawn(inner);
   223	
   224	  #[cfg(any(
   225	    all(target_family = "wasm", tokio_unstable),
   226	    not(target_family = "wasm")
   227	  ))]
   228	  spawn(async move {
   229	    if let Err(err) = jh.await {
   230	      if let Ok(reason) = err.try_into_panic() {
   231	        if let Some(s) = reason.downcast_ref::<&str>() {
   232	          deferred_for_panic.reject(Error::new(crate::Status::GenericFailure, s));
   233	        } else {
   234	          deferred_for_panic.reject(Error::new(
   235	            crate::Status::GenericFailure,
   236	            "Panic in async function",
   237	          ));
   238	        }
   239	      }
   240	    }

   256	pub fn execute_tokio_future_with_finalize_callback<
   257	  Data: 'static + Send,
   258	  Fut: 'static + Send + Future<Output = std::result::Result<Data, impl Into<Error>>>,
   259	  Resolver: 'static + FnOnce(sys::napi_env, Data) -> Result<sys::napi_value>,
   260	>(
   261	  env: sys::napi_env,
   262	  fut: Fut,
   263	  resolver: Resolver,
   264	  finalize_callback: Option<Box<dyn FnOnce(sys::napi_env)>>,
   265	) -> Result<sys::napi_value> {
   266	  let env = Env::from_raw(env);
   267	  let (mut deferred, promise) = JsDeferred::new(&env)?;
   268	  deferred.set_finalize_callback(finalize_callback);
   269	  #[cfg(any(
   270	    all(target_family = "wasm", tokio_unstable),
   271	    not(target_family = "wasm")
   272	  ))]
   273	  let deferred_for_panic = deferred.clone();
   274	  let sendable_resolver = SendableResolver::new(resolver);
   275	
   276	  let inner = async move {
   277	    match fut.await {
   278	      Ok(v) => deferred.resolve(move |env| {
   279	        sendable_resolver
   280	          .resolve(env.raw(), v)
   281	          .map(|v| unsafe { Unknown::from_raw_unchecked(env.raw(), v) })
   282	      }),
   283	      Err(e) => deferred.reject(e.into()),
   284	    }
   285	  };
   286	
   287	  #[cfg(any(
   288	    all(target_family = "wasm", tokio_unstable),
   289	    not(target_family = "wasm")
   290	  ))]
   291	  let jh = spawn(inner);
   292	
   293	  #[cfg(any(
   294	    all(target_family = "wasm", tokio_unstable),
   295	    not(target_family = "wasm")
   296	  ))]
   297	  spawn(async move {
   298	    if let Err(err) = jh.await {
   299	      if let Ok(reason) = err.try_into_panic() {
   300	        if let Some(s) = reason.downcast_ref::<&str>() {
   301	          deferred_for_panic.reject(Error::new(crate::Status::GenericFailure, s));
   302	        } else {
   303	          deferred_for_panic.reject(Error::new(
   304	            crate::Status::GenericFailure,
   305	            "Panic in async function",
   306	          ));

   308	      }
   309	    }
   310	  });
   311	
   312	  #[cfg(all(target_family = "wasm", not(tokio_unstable)))]

   317	  }

   574	fn napi_fn_from_decl(
   575	  sig: &mut Signature,
   576	  opts: &BindgenAttrs,
   577	  attrs: Vec<Attribute>,
   578	  vis: Visibility,
   579	  parent: Option<&Ident>,
   580	  parent_js_name: Option<String>,
   581	) -> BindgenResult<NapiFn> {
   582	  let mut errors = vec![];
   583	
   584	  let syn::Signature {
   585	    ident,
   586	    asyncness,
   587	    output,
   588	    generics,
   589	    ..
   590	  } = sig.clone();
   591	
   592	  let mut fn_self = None;
   593	  let callback_traits = extract_fn_closure_generics(&generics)?;
   594	
   595	  let args = sig
   596	    .inputs
   597	    .iter_mut()
   598	    .filter_map(|arg| match arg {
   599	      syn::FnArg::Typed(ref mut p) => {
   600	        let ts_arg_type = find_ts_arg_type_and_remove_attribute(p, opts.ts_args_type().as_ref())
   601	          .unwrap_or_else(|e| {
   602	            errors.push(e);
   603	            None
   604	          });
   605	
   606	        let ty_str = p.ty.to_token_stream().to_string();
   607	        if let Some(path_arguments) = callback_traits.get(&ty_str) {
   608	          match extract_callback_trait_types(path_arguments) {
   609	            Ok((fn_args, fn_ret)) => Some(NapiFnArg {
   610	              kind: NapiFnArgKind::Callback(Box::new(CallbackArg {
   611	                pat: p.pat.clone(),
   612	                args: fn_args,
   613	                ret: fn_ret,
   614	              })),
   615	              ts_arg_type,
   616	            }),
   617	            Err(e) => {
   618	              errors.push(e);
   619	              None
   620	            }
   621	          }
   622	        } else {
   623	          let ty = replace_self(p.ty.as_ref().clone(), parent);
   624	          *p.ty = ty;
   625	          Some(NapiFnArg {
   626	            kind: NapiFnArgKind::PatType(Box::new(p.clone())),
   627	            ts_arg_type,
   628	          })
   629	        }
   630	      }
   631	      syn::FnArg::Receiver(r) => {
   632	        if parent.is_some() {
   633	          assert!(fn_self.is_none());
   634	          if r.reference.is_none() {
   635	            errors.push(err_span!(
   636	              r,
   637	              "The native methods can't move values from napi. Try `&self` or `&mut self` instead."
   638	            ));
   639	          } else if r.mutability.is_some() {
   640	            fn_self = Some(FnSelf::MutRef);
   641	          } else {
   642	            fn_self = Some(FnSelf::Ref);
   643	          }
   644	        } else {
   645	          errors.push(err_span!(r, "arguments cannot be `self`"));
   646	        }
   647	        None
   648	      }
   649	    })
   650	    .collect::<Vec<_>>();
   651	
   652	  let (ret, is_ret_result) = match output {
   653	    syn::ReturnType::Default => (None, false),
   654	    syn::ReturnType::Type(_, ty) => {
   655	      let result_ty = extract_result_ty(&ty)?;
   656	      if let Some(result_ty) = result_ty {
   657	        (Some(replace_self(result_ty, parent)), true)
   658	      } else {
   659	        (Some(replace_self(*ty, parent)), false)
   660	      }
   661	    }
   662	  };
   663	
   664	  Diagnostic::from_vec(errors).and_then(|_| {
   665	    let js_name = if let Some(prop_name) = opts.getter() {
   666	      opts.js_name().map_or_else(
   667	        || {
   668	          if let Some(ident) = prop_name {
   669	            ident.to_string()
   670	          } else {
   671	            to_case(ident.to_string().trim_start_matches("get_"), Case::Camel)
   672	          }
   673	        },
   674	        |(js_name, _)| js_name.to_owned(),
   675	      )
   676	    } else if let Some(prop_name) = opts.setter() {
   677	      opts.js_name().map_or_else(
   678	        || {
   679	          if let Some(ident) = prop_name {
   680	            ident.to_string()
   681	          } else {
   682	            to_case(ident.to_string().trim_start_matches("set_"), Case::Camel)
   683	          }
   684	        },
   685	        |(js_name, _)| js_name.to_owned(),
   686	      )
   687	    } else if opts.constructor().is_some() {
   688	      "constructor".to_owned()
   689	    } else if opts.module_exports().is_some() {
   690	      if opts.js_name().is_some() {
   691	        bail_span!(sig.ident, "module_exports fn can't have js_name");
   692	      }
   693	      if opts.getter().is_some() || opts.setter().is_some() {
   694	        bail_span!(sig.ident, "module_exports fn can't have getter or setter");
   695	      }
   696	      if opts.factory().is_some() || opts.constructor().is_some() {
   697	        bail_span!(
   698	          sig.ident,
   699	          "module_exports fn can't have factory or constructor"
   700	        );
   701	      }
   702	      if opts.strict().is_some() {
   703	        bail_span!(sig.ident, "module_exports fn can't have strict");
   704	      }
   705	      if opts.return_if_invalid().is_some() {
   706	        bail_span!(sig.ident, "module_exports fn can't have return_if_invalid");
   707	      }
   708	
   709	      if parent.is_some() {
   710	        bail_span!(sig.ident, "module_exports fn can't inside impl block");
   711	      }
   712	
   713	      if !generics.params.is_empty() {
   714	        bail_span!(sig.ident, "module_exports fn can't have generic parameters");
   715	      }
   716	
   717	      if opts.no_export().is_some() {
   718	        bail_span!(
   719	          sig.ident,
   720	          "#[napi(no_export)] can not be used with module_exports attribute"
   721	        );
   722	      }
   723	
   724	      for arg in args.iter() {
   725	        match &arg.kind {
   726	          NapiFnArgKind::Callback(_) => {
   727	            bail_span!(sig.ident, "module_exports fn can't have callback arguments");
   728	          }
   729	          NapiFnArgKind::PatType(pat) => {
   730	            if arg.ts_arg_type.is_some() {
   731	              bail_span!(sig.ident, "module_exports fn can't have ts_arg_type");
   732	            }
   733	            if let syn::Type::Path(syn::TypePath {
   734	              path: syn::Path { segments, .. },
   735	              ..
   736	            }) = &*pat.ty
   737	            {
   738	              if let Some(segment) = segments.last() {
   739	                if segment.ident != "Env" && segment.ident != "Object" {
   740	                  bail_span!(
   741	                    sig.ident,
   742	                    "module_exports fn can only accept Env or Object as argument"
   743	                  );
   744	                }
   745	                continue;
   746	              }
   747	            }
   748	            if let syn::Type::Reference(syn::TypeReference { elem, .. }) = &*pat.ty {
   749	              if let syn::Type::Path(syn::TypePath {
   750	                path: syn::Path { segments, .. },
   751	                ..
   752	              }) = &**elem
   753	              {
   754	                if let Some(segment) = segments.last() {
   755	                  if segment.ident != "Env" && segment.ident != "Object" {
   756	                    bail_span!(
   757	                      sig.ident,
   758	                      "module_exports fn can only accept Env or Object as argument"
   759	                    );
   760	                  }
   761	                  continue;
   762	                }
   763	              }
   764	            }
   765	          }
   766	        }
   767	        bail_span!(
   768	          sig.ident,
   769	          "module_exports fn can only accept Env or Object as argument"
   770	        );
   771	      }
   772	
   773	      if let syn::ReturnType::Type(_, ty) = &sig.output {
   774	        if let syn::Type::Path(syn::TypePath {
   775	          path: syn::Path { segments, .. },
   776	          ..
   777	        }) = &**ty
   778	        {
   779	          if let Some(segment) = segments.last() {
   780	            if segment.ident != "Result" && segment.ident != "()" {
   781	              bail_span!(
   782	                sig.ident,
   783	                "module_exports fn can only return Result<()> or (), got {}",
   784	                segment.ident
   785	              );
   786	            }
   787	            if segment.ident == "Result" {
   788	              if let syn::PathArguments::AngleBracketed(syn::AngleBracketedGenericArguments {
   789	                args,
   790	                ..
   791	              }) = &segment.arguments
   792	              {
   793	                if args.len() != 1 {
   794	                  bail_span!(
   795	                    segment.ident,
   796	                    "module_exports fn can only return Result<()> or ()"
   797	                  );
   798	                }
   799	                if let syn::GenericArgument::Type(syn::Type::Tuple(syn::TypeTuple {
   800	                  elems, ..
   801	                })) = &args[0]
   802	                {
   803	                  if !elems.empty_or_trailing() {
   804	                    bail_span!(
   805	                      segment.ident,
   806	                      "module_exports fn can only return Result<()> or ()"
   807	                    );
   808	                  }
   809	                } else {
   810	                  bail_span!(
   811	                    segment.ident,
   812	                    "module_exports fn can only return Result<()> or ()"
   813	                  );
   814	                }
   815	              } else {
   816	                bail_span!(
   817	                  segment.ident,
   818	                  "module_exports fn can only return Result<()> or ()"
   819	                );
   820	              }
   821	            }
   822	          }
   823	        }
   824	      }
   825	
   826	      to_case(ident.to_string(), Case::Camel)
   827	    } else {
   828	      opts.js_name().map_or_else(
   829	        || to_case(ident.to_string(), Case::Camel),
   830	        |(js_name, _)| js_name.to_owned(),
   831	      )
   832	    };
   833	
   834	    let namespace = opts.namespace().map(|(m, _)| m.to_owned());
   835	    let (parent_is_generator, parent_is_async_generator) = if let Some(p) = parent {
   836	      let generator_struct = GENERATOR_STRUCT.get_or_init(|| Mutex::new(HashMap::new()));
   837	      let generator_struct = generator_struct
   838	        .lock()
   839	        .expect("Lock generator struct failed");
   840	
   841	      let key = namespace
   842	        .as_ref()
   843	        .map(|n| format!("{n}::{p}"))
   844	        .unwrap_or_else(|| p.to_string());
   845	      *generator_struct.get(&key).unwrap_or(&(false, false))
   846	    } else {
   847	      (false, false)
   848	    };
   849	
   850	    let kind = fn_kind(opts);
   851	
   852	    if !matches!(kind, FnKind::Normal) && parent.is_none() {
   853	      bail_span!(
   854	        sig.ident,
   855	        "Only fn in impl block can be marked as factory, constructor, getter or setter"
   856	      );
   857	    }
   858	
   859	    if matches!(kind, FnKind::Constructor) && asyncness.is_some() {
   860	      bail_span!(sig.ident, "Constructor don't support asynchronous function");
   861	    }
   862	
   863	    Ok(NapiFn {
   864	      name: ident.clone(),
   865	      js_name,
   866	      module_exports: opts.module_exports().is_some(),
   867	      args,
   868	      ret,
   869	      is_ret_result,
   870	      is_async: asyncness.is_some(),
   871	      within_async_runtime: opts.async_runtime().is_some(),
   872	      vis,
   873	      kind,
   874	      fn_self,
   875	      parent: parent.cloned(),
   876	      parent_js_name,
   877	      comments: extract_doc_comments(&attrs),
   878	      attrs,
   879	      strict: opts.strict().is_some(),
   880	      return_if_invalid: opts.return_if_invalid().is_some(),
   881	      js_mod: opts.namespace().map(|(m, _)| m.to_owned()),
   882	      ts_type: opts.ts_type().map(|(m, _)| m.to_owned()),
   883	      ts_generic_types: opts.ts_generic_types().map(|(m, _)| m.to_owned()),
   884	      ts_args_type: opts.ts_args_type().map(|(m, _)| m.to_owned()),
   885	      ts_return_type: opts.ts_return_type().map(|(m, _)| m.to_owned()),
   886	      skip_typescript: opts.skip_typescript().is_some(),
   887	      parent_is_generator,
   888	      parent_is_async_generator,
   889	      writable: opts.writable(),
   890	      enumerable: opts.enumerable(),
   891	      configurable: opts.configurable(),
   892	      catch_unwind: opts.catch_unwind().is_some(),
   893	      unsafe_: sig.unsafety.is_some(),
   894	      register_name: get_register_ident(ident.to_string().as_str()),
   895	      no_export: opts.no_export().is_some(),
   896	    })
   897	  })
   898	}

   900	impl ParseNapi for syn::Item {

     1	use super::typedef;
     2	use crate::parser::{attrs::BindgenAttrs, ParseNapi};
     3	use napi_derive_backend::{BindgenResult, TryToTokens};
     4	use proc_macro2::TokenStream;
     5	use quote::ToTokens;
     6	use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
     7	use syn::{Attribute, Item};
     8	
     9	/// a flag indicate whether or never at least one `napi` macro has been expanded.
    10	/// ```ignore
    11	/// if BUILT_FLAG
    12	///  .compare_exchange(false, true, Ordering::Acquire, Ordering::Relaxed)
    13	///  .is_ok() {
    14	///   // logic on first macro expansion
    15	/// }
    16	///
    17	/// ```
    18	static BUILT_FLAG: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
    19	
    20	pub fn expand(attr: TokenStream, input: TokenStream) -> BindgenResult<TokenStream> {
    21	  // logic on first macro expansion
    22	  if let Ok(built) = BUILT_FLAG.compare_exchange(false, true, Ordering::Acquire, Ordering::Relaxed)
    23	  {
    24	    if !built {
    25	      typedef::prepare_type_def_file();
    26	    }
    27	  }
    28	
    29	  let mut item = syn::parse2::<Item>(input)?;
    30	  let opts: BindgenAttrs = syn::parse2(attr)?;
    31	  let mut tokens = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();
    32	  if let Item::Mod(mut js_mod) = item {
    33	    let js_name = opts.js_name().map_or_else(
    34	      || js_mod.ident.to_string(),
    35	      |(js_name, _)| js_name.to_owned(),
    36	    );
    37	    if let Some((_, mut items)) = js_mod.content.clone() {
    38	      for item in items.iter_mut() {
    39	        let mut empty_attrs = vec![];
    40	        if let Some(item_opts) = replace_napi_attr_in_mod(
    41	          js_name.clone(),
    42	          match item {
    43	            Item::Fn(ref mut function) => &mut function.attrs,
    44	            Item::Struct(ref mut struct_) => &mut struct_.attrs,
    45	            Item::Enum(ref mut enum_) => &mut enum_.attrs,
    46	            Item::Const(ref mut const_) => &mut const_.attrs,
    47	            Item::Impl(ref mut impl_) => &mut impl_.attrs,
    48	            Item::Mod(mod_) => {
    49	              let mod_in_mod = mod_
    50	                .attrs
    51	                .iter()
    52	                .enumerate()
    53	                .find(|(_, m)| m.path().is_ident("napi"));
    54	              if mod_in_mod.is_some() {
    55	                bail_span!(
    56	                  mod_,
    57	                  "napi module cannot be nested under another napi module"
    58	                );
    59	              } else {
    60	                &mut empty_attrs
    61	              }
    62	            }
    63	            _ => &mut empty_attrs,
    64	          },
    65	        ) {
    66	          let napi = item.parse_napi(&mut tokens, &item_opts)?;
    67	          item_opts.check_used()?;
    68	          napi.try_to_tokens(&mut tokens)?;
    69	          typedef::output_type_def(&napi);
    70	        } else {
    71	          item.to_tokens(&mut tokens);
    72	        };
    73	      }
    74	      js_mod.content = None;
    75	    };
    76	
    77	    let js_mod_attrs: Vec<Attribute> = js_mod
    78	      .attrs
    79	      .clone()
    80	      .into_iter()
    81	      .filter(|attr| attr.path().is_ident("napi"))
    82	      .collect();
    83	    let mod_name = js_mod.ident;
    84	    let visible = js_mod.vis;
    85	    let mod_tokens = quote! { #(#js_mod_attrs)* #visible mod #mod_name { #tokens } };
    86	    Ok(mod_tokens)
    87	  } else {
    88	    let napi = item.parse_napi(&mut tokens, &opts)?;
    89	    opts.check_used()?;
    90	    napi.try_to_tokens(&mut tokens)?;
    91	    typedef::output_type_def(&napi);
    92	    Ok(tokens)
    93	  }
    94	}
    95	
    96	fn replace_napi_attr_in_mod(
    97	  js_namespace: String,
    98	  attrs: &mut Vec<syn::Attribute>,
    99	) -> Option<BindgenAttrs> {
   100	  let napi_attr = attrs
   101	    .iter()
